				unitskilluseid getcharid(3), "MG_FIREBOLT", getskilllv("MG_FIREBOLT"), @AUTO_CURRENT_TARGET, 0, 0, 0, 0;
				.@step_delay = 900; addtimer .@step_delay, "AutoPlayerSystem::OnStepTimer"; end;
			} else if (.@sk_p$ == "MG_COLDBOLT" && getskilllv("MG_COLDBOLT") > 0 && Sp >= 12) {
				unitskilluseid getcharid(3), "MG_COLDBOLT", getskilllv("MG_COLDBOLT"), @AUTO_CURRENT_TARGET, 0, 0, 0, 0;
				.@step_delay = 900; addtimer .@step_delay, "AutoPlayerSystem::OnStepTimer"; end;
			} else if (.@sk_p$ == "MG_LIGHTNINGBOLT" && getskilllv("MG_LIGHTNINGBOLT") > 0 && Sp >= 12) {
				unitskilluseid getcharid(3), "MG_LIGHTNINGBOLT", getskilllv("MG_LIGHTNINGBOLT"), @AUTO_CURRENT_TARGET, 0, 0, 0, 0;
				.@step_delay = 900; addtimer .@step_delay, "AutoPlayerSystem::OnStepTimer"; end;
			} else if (.@sk_p$ == "MG_SOULSTRIKE" && getskilllv("MG_SOULSTRIKE") > 0 && Sp >= 15) {
				unitskilluseid getcharid(3), "MG_SOULSTRIKE", getskilllv("MG_SOULSTRIKE"), @AUTO_CURRENT_TARGET, 0, 0, 0, 0;
				.@step_delay = 600; addtimer .@step_delay, "AutoPlayerSystem::OnStepTimer"; end;
			}
		}

		// ==============================================================
		// Feiticeiro / Sorcerer / Professor / Sabio:
		// ==============================================================
		.@is_sorc_class = (Class == Job_Sorcerer || Class == Job_Sorcerer_T || Class == 4074 || Class == 4067 || Class == Job_Baby_Sorcerer || Class == Job_Elemental_Master || Class == Job_Elemental_Master_T || Class == Job_Baby_Elemental_Master || Class == 4255 || BaseJob == Job_Sorcerer);
		.@has_sorc_skills = (getskilllv("SO_PSYCHIC_WAVE") > 0 || getskilllv("SO_POISON_BUSTER") > 0 || getskilllv("SO_DIAMONDDUST") > 0 || getskilllv("SO_EARTHGRAVE") > 0 || getskilllv("SO_VARETYR_SPEAR") > 0 || getskilllv("SO_CLOUD_KILL") > 0);

		// Se for Feiticeiro e JA tiver habilidades de 3a classe aprendidas:
		if (.@is_sorc_class && .@has_sorc_skills) {
			// Prioridade 1: Se Onda Psiquica estiver lvl max (>= 5), spama sempre que disponivel (sem cooldown artificial e sem depender de outras)
			if (getskilllv("SO_PSYCHIC_WAVE") >= 5 && Sp >= 70 && .@now >= @CD_SO_PSYCHIC_WAVE) {
				unitskillusepos getcharid(3), "SO_PSYCHIC_WAVE", getskilllv("SO_PSYCHIC_WAVE"), .@mx, .@my;
				.@step_delay = 450;
				addtimer .@step_delay, "AutoPlayerSystem::OnStepTimer";
				end;
			}
			// Prioridade 2: Se Implosao Toxica estiver lvl max (>= 5) e Onda nao, spama Implosao sempre que disponivel
			else if (getskilllv("SO_POISON_BUSTER") >= 5 && (getskilllv("SO_PSYCHIC_WAVE") < 5 || .@now < @CD_SO_PSYCHIC_WAVE) && Sp >= 70 && .@now >= @CD_SO_POISON_BUSTER) {
				unitskilluseid getcharid(3), "SO_POISON_BUSTER", getskilllv("SO_POISON_BUSTER"), @AUTO_CURRENT_TARGET, 0, 0, 0, 0;
				.@step_delay = 450;
				addtimer .@step_delay, "AutoPlayerSystem::OnStepTimer";
				end;
			}
			// Prioridade 3: Se Po de Diamante estiver lvl max (>= 5) e Onda e Implosao nao, spama Po de Diamante sempre que disponivel
			else if (getskilllv("SO_DIAMONDDUST") >= 5 && (getskilllv("SO_PSYCHIC_WAVE") < 5 || .@now < @CD_SO_PSYCHIC_WAVE) && (getskilllv("SO_POISON_BUSTER") < 5 || .@now < @CD_SO_POISON_BUSTER) && Sp >= 50 && .@now >= @CD_SO_DIAMONDDUST) {
				unitskillusepos getcharid(3), "SO_DIAMONDDUST", getskilllv("SO_DIAMONDDUST"), .@mx, .@my;
				.@step_delay = 450;
				addtimer .@step_delay, "AutoPlayerSystem::OnStepTimer";
				end;
			}
			// Enquanto a prioritaria estiver em cooldown (ou nenhuma das 3 estiver lvl max), usa as outras habilidades aprendidas em rotacao:
			else {
				for (.@siter = 0; .@siter < 6; .@siter++) {
					.@sidx = (@SO_ROT_STEP + .@siter) % 6;
					if (.@sidx == 0 && getskilllv("SO_PSYCHIC_WAVE") > 0 && Sp >= 70 && .@now >= @CD_SO_PSYCHIC_WAVE) {
						@SO_ROT_STEP = (.@sidx + 1) % 6;
						unitskillusepos getcharid(3), "SO_PSYCHIC_WAVE", getskilllv("SO_PSYCHIC_WAVE"), .@mx, .@my;
						.@step_delay = 450;
						addtimer .@step_delay, "AutoPlayerSystem::OnStepTimer";
						end;
					} else if (.@sidx == 1 && getskilllv("SO_POISON_BUSTER") > 0 && Sp >= 70 && .@now >= @CD_SO_POISON_BUSTER) {
						@SO_ROT_STEP = (.@sidx + 1) % 6;
						unitskilluseid getcharid(3), "SO_POISON_BUSTER", getskilllv("SO_POISON_BUSTER"), @AUTO_CURRENT_TARGET, 0, 0, 0, 0;
						.@step_delay = 450;
						addtimer .@step_delay, "AutoPlayerSystem::OnStepTimer";
						end;
					} else if (.@sidx == 2 && getskilllv("SO_DIAMONDDUST") > 0 && Sp >= 50 && .@now >= @CD_SO_DIAMONDDUST) {
						@SO_ROT_STEP = (.@sidx + 1) % 6;
						unitskillusepos getcharid(3), "SO_DIAMONDDUST", getskilllv("SO_DIAMONDDUST"), .@mx, .@my;
						.@step_delay = 450;
						addtimer .@step_delay, "AutoPlayerSystem::OnStepTimer";
						end;
					} else if (.@sidx == 3 && getskilllv("SO_EARTHGRAVE") > 0 && Sp >= 62) {
						@SO_ROT_STEP = (.@sidx + 1) % 6;
						unitskillusepos getcharid(3), "SO_EARTHGRAVE", getskilllv("SO_EARTHGRAVE"), .@mx, .@my;
						.@step_delay = 450;
						addtimer .@step_delay, "AutoPlayerSystem::OnStepTimer";
						end;
					} else if (.@sidx == 4 && getskilllv("SO_VARETYR_SPEAR") > 0 && Sp >= 65) {
						@SO_ROT_STEP = (.@sidx + 1) % 6;
						unitskilluseid getcharid(3), "SO_VARETYR_SPEAR", getskilllv("SO_VARETYR_SPEAR"), @AUTO_CURRENT_TARGET, 0, 0, 0, 0;
						.@step_delay = 450;
						addtimer .@step_delay, "AutoPlayerSystem::OnStepTimer";
						end;
					} else if (.@sidx == 5 && getskilllv("SO_CLOUD_KILL") > 0 && Sp >= 48) {
						@SO_ROT_STEP = (.@sidx + 1) % 6;
						unitskillusepos getcharid(3), "SO_CLOUD_KILL", getskilllv("SO_CLOUD_KILL"), .@mx, .@my;
						.@step_delay = 450;
						addtimer .@step_delay, "AutoPlayerSystem::OnStepTimer";
						end;
					}
				}
			}
		}

		// Heranca de 2nd T (Professor / Sabio) para Feiticeiro (enquanto sem skills de 3a classe ou durante cooldown):
		.@is_sage_tree = (.@is_sorc_class || Class == Job_Sage || Class == Job_Sage_T || Class == Job_Baby_Sage || Class == Job_Professor || Class == Job_Baby_Professor || Class == 4017 || Class == 16 || BaseJob == Job_Sage || BaseJob == Job_Professor || getskilllv("PF_DOUBLECASTING") > 0);
		if (.@is_sage_tree) {
			// Lancas Duplas (PF_DOUBLECASTING) - Apenas se usar lancas elementais e NAO tiver habilidades de 3a classe
			if (!.@has_sorc_skills && getskilllv("PF_DOUBLECASTING") > 0 && Sp >= 40 && !getstatus(SC_DOUBLECAST) && .@now >= @BUFF_PF_DOUBLECAST + 5 && (getskilllv("MG_FIREBOLT") > 0 || getskilllv("MG_COLDBOLT") > 0 || getskilllv("MG_LIGHTNINGBOLT") > 0)) {
				@BUFF_PF_DOUBLECAST = .@now + 90;
				unitskilluseid getcharid(3), "PF_DOUBLECASTING", getskilllv("PF_DOUBLECASTING"), getcharid(3), 0, 0, 0, 0;
				.@step_delay = 1800;
				addtimer .@step_delay, "AutoPlayerSystem::OnStepTimer";
				end;
			}

			// Furia da Terra / Coluna de Pedra / Lancas Elementais
			if (getskilllv("WZ_HEAVENDRIVE") > 0 && Sp >= 28 && ((.@now - @AUTO_LAST_HEAVENDRIVE) >= 2 || getskilllv("MG_FIREBOLT") <= 0)) {
				@AUTO_LAST_HEAVENDRIVE = .@now;
				unitskillusepos getcharid(3), "WZ_HEAVENDRIVE", getskilllv("WZ_HEAVENDRIVE"), .@mx, .@my;
				.@step_delay = 1000;
				addtimer .@step_delay, "AutoPlayerSystem::OnStepTimer";
				end;
			} else if (getskilllv("WZ_EARTHSPIKE") > 0 && Sp >= 12 && ((@AUTO_SKILL_ROT % 3 == 0) || (getskilllv("MG_FIREBOLT") <= 0 && getskilllv("MG_COLDBOLT") <= 0 && getskilllv("MG_LIGHTNINGBOLT") <= 0))) {
				unitskilluseid getcharid(3), "WZ_EARTHSPIKE", getskilllv("WZ_EARTHSPIKE"), @AUTO_CURRENT_TARGET, 0, 0, 0, 0;
				.@step_delay = 700;
				addtimer .@step_delay, "AutoPlayerSystem::OnStepTimer";
				end;
			} else {
				deletearray .@prof_bolts$[0], 3;
				.@bolt_cnt = 0;
				if (getskilllv("MG_FIREBOLT") > 0 && Sp >= 12) {
					.@prof_bolts$[.@bolt_cnt] = "MG_FIREBOLT";
					.@bolt_cnt++;
				}
				if (getskilllv("MG_COLDBOLT") > 0 && Sp >= 12) {
					.@prof_bolts$[.@bolt_cnt] = "MG_COLDBOLT";
					.@bolt_cnt++;
				}
				if (getskilllv("MG_LIGHTNINGBOLT") > 0 && Sp >= 12) {
					.@prof_bolts$[.@bolt_cnt] = "MG_LIGHTNINGBOLT";
					.@bolt_cnt++;
				}
				if (.@bolt_cnt > 0) {
					.@chosen_bolt$ = .@prof_bolts$[@AUTO_SKILL_ROT % .@bolt_cnt];
					unitskilluseid getcharid(3), .@chosen_bolt$, getskilllv(.@chosen_bolt$), @AUTO_CURRENT_TARGET, 0, 0, 0, 0;
					.@step_delay = 800;
					addtimer .@step_delay, "AutoPlayerSystem::OnStepTimer";
					end;
				} else if (getskilllv("MG_SOULSTRIKE") > 0 && Sp >= 15) {
					unitskilluseid getcharid(3), "MG_SOULSTRIKE", getskilllv("MG_SOULSTRIKE"), @AUTO_CURRENT_TARGET, 0, 0, 0, 0;
					.@step_delay = 600;
					addtimer .@step_delay, "AutoPlayerSystem::OnStepTimer";
					end;
				}
			}
		}

		// Arcano / Warlock: Habilidades de 3a classe
		.@is_wl = (Class == Job_Warlock || Class == Job_Warlock_T || Class == Job_Baby_Warlock || Class == Job_Arch_Mage || Class == Job_Arch_Mage_T || Class == Job_Baby_Arch_Mage || getskilllv("WL_CHAINLIGHTNING") > 0 || getskilllv("WL_CRIMSONROCK") > 0 || getskilllv("WL_HELLINFERNO") > 0 || getskilllv("WL_COMET") > 0 || getskilllv("WL_SOULEXPANSION") > 0 || getskilllv("WL_EARTHSTRAIN") > 0 || getskilllv("WL_JACKFROST") > 0);
		if (.@is_wl) {
			// Regra 1: Se Cometa estiver lvl maximo (>= 5), priorize Cometa enquanto nao estiver em cooldown
			if (getskilllv("WL_COMET") >= 5 && Sp >= 150 && .@now >= @CD_WL_COMET) {
				@CD_WL_COMET = .@now + 20;
				unitskillusepos getcharid(3), "WL_COMET", getskilllv("WL_COMET"), .@mx, .@my;
				.@step_delay = 1100;
				addtimer .@step_delay, "AutoPlayerSystem::OnStepTimer";
				end;
			}
			// Regra 2: Se Impacto Espiritual estiver lvl maximo (>= 5) e Cometa nao estiver maximo (< 5), priorize Impacto Espiritual
			else if (getskilllv("WL_SOULEXPANSION") >= 5 && getskilllv("WL_COMET") < 5 && Sp >= 50) {
				unitskilluseid getcharid(3), "WL_SOULEXPANSION", getskilllv("WL_SOULEXPANSION"), @AUTO_CURRENT_TARGET, 0, 0, 0, 0;
				.@step_delay = 500;
				addtimer .@step_delay, "AutoPlayerSystem::OnStepTimer";
				end;
			}
			// Regra 3: Rotacao sequencial - Chamas de Hela, Abalo Sismico, Corrente Eletrica, Meteoro Escarlate e Esquife de Gelo
			else {
				for (.@iter = 0; .@iter < 5; .@iter++) {
					.@idx = (@WL_ROT_STEP + .@iter) % 5;
					if (.@idx == 0 && getskilllv("WL_HELLINFERNO") > 0 && Sp >= 64 && .@now >= @CD_WL_HELLINFERNO) {
						@CD_WL_HELLINFERNO = .@now + 3;
						@WL_ROT_STEP = (.@idx + 1) % 5;
						unitskilluseid getcharid(3), "WL_HELLINFERNO", getskilllv("WL_HELLINFERNO"), @AUTO_CURRENT_TARGET, 0, 0, 0, 0;
						.@step_delay = 600;
						addtimer .@step_delay, "AutoPlayerSystem::OnStepTimer";
						end;
					} else if (.@idx == 1 && getskilllv("WL_EARTHSTRAIN") > 0 && Sp >= 70 && .@dist <= 6 && .@now >= @CD_WL_EARTHSTRAIN) {
						@CD_WL_EARTHSTRAIN = .@now + 7;
						@WL_ROT_STEP = (.@idx + 1) % 5;
						unitskillusepos getcharid(3), "WL_EARTHSTRAIN", getskilllv("WL_EARTHSTRAIN"), .@mx, .@my;
						.@step_delay = 650;
						addtimer .@step_delay, "AutoPlayerSystem::OnStepTimer";
						end;
					} else if (.@idx == 2 && getskilllv("WL_CHAINLIGHTNING") > 0 && Sp >= 80) {
						@WL_ROT_STEP = (.@idx + 1) % 5;
						unitskilluseid getcharid(3), "WL_CHAINLIGHTNING", getskilllv("WL_CHAINLIGHTNING"), @AUTO_CURRENT_TARGET, 0, 0, 0, 0;
						.@step_delay = 600;
						addtimer .@step_delay, "AutoPlayerSystem::OnStepTimer";
						end;
					} else if (.@idx == 3 && getskilllv("WL_CRIMSONROCK") > 0 && Sp >= 60 && .@now >= @CD_WL_CRIMSONROCK) {
						@CD_WL_CRIMSONROCK = .@now + 5;
						@WL_ROT_STEP = (.@idx + 1) % 5;
						unitskilluseid getcharid(3), "WL_CRIMSONROCK", getskilllv("WL_CRIMSONROCK"), @AUTO_CURRENT_TARGET, 0, 0, 0, 0;
						.@step_delay = 650;
						addtimer .@step_delay, "AutoPlayerSystem::OnStepTimer";
						end;
					} else if (.@idx == 4 && getskilllv("WL_JACKFROST") > 0 && Sp >= 50 && .@now >= @CD_WL_JACKFROST) {
						@CD_WL_JACKFROST = .@now + 4;
						@WL_ROT_STEP = (.@idx + 1) % 5;
						unitskilluseid getcharid(3), "WL_JACKFROST", getskilllv("WL_JACKFROST"), @AUTO_CURRENT_TARGET, 0, 0, 0, 0;
						.@step_delay = 600;
						addtimer .@step_delay, "AutoPlayerSystem::OnStepTimer";
						end;
					}
				}
			}
		}

		// Arquimago / Bruxo / Warlock sem skills de 3a classe:
			.@is_wz = (Class == Job_Wizard || Class == Job_Wizard_T || Class == Job_High_Wizard || Class == Job_High_Wizard_T || Class == Job_Baby_Wizard || Class == Job_Warlock || Class == Job_Warlock_T || Class == Job_Baby_Warlock || Class == Job_Arch_Mage || Class == Job_Arch_Mage_T || Class == Job_Baby_Arch_Mage || BaseJob == Job_Wizard || BaseJob == Job_High_Wizard || BaseJob == Job_Warlock || getskilllv("WZ_STORMGUST") > 0 || getskilllv("WZ_VERMILION") > 0 || getskilllv("WZ_METEOR") > 0 || getskilllv("WZ_HEAVENDRIVE") > 0 || getskilllv("HW_NAPALMVULCAN") > 0);

			if (.@is_wz && (getskilllv("WZ_STORMGUST") > 0 || getskilllv("WZ_VERMILION") > 0 || getskilllv("WZ_METEOR") > 0 || getskilllv("WZ_HEAVENDRIVE") > 0 || getskilllv("HW_NAPALMVULCAN") > 0)) {
				// Alterna com Vulcao Napalm (se tiver e rot % 2 == 0 ou se nao tiver magias em area)
				if (getskilllv("HW_NAPALMVULCAN") > 0 && Sp >= 50 && ((@AUTO_SKILL_ROT % 2 == 0) || (getskilllv("WZ_STORMGUST") <= 0 && getskilllv("WZ_VERMILION") <= 0 && getskilllv("WZ_METEOR") <= 0 && getskilllv("WZ_HEAVENDRIVE") <= 0))) {
					unitskilluseid getcharid(3), "HW_NAPALMVULCAN", getskilllv("HW_NAPALMVULCAN"), @AUTO_CURRENT_TARGET, 0, 0, 0, 0;
					.@step_delay = 600;
					addtimer .@step_delay, "AutoPlayerSystem::OnStepTimer";
					end;
				} else {
					// Habilidades em Area do Bruxo/Arquimago: Nevasca, Ira de Thor, Furia da Terra e Chuva de Meteoros
					deletearray .@wz_skills$[0], 4;
					.@wz_count = 0;
					if (getskilllv("WZ_STORMGUST") > 0 && Sp >= 40) {
						.@wz_skills$[.@wz_count] = "WZ_STORMGUST";
						.@wz_count++;
					}
					if (getskilllv("WZ_VERMILION") > 0 && Sp >= 40) {
						.@wz_skills$[.@wz_count] = "WZ_VERMILION";
						.@wz_count++;
					}
					if (getskilllv("WZ_HEAVENDRIVE") > 0 && Sp >= 28) {
						.@wz_skills$[.@wz_count] = "WZ_HEAVENDRIVE";
						.@wz_count++;
					}
					if (getskilllv("WZ_METEOR") > 0 && Sp >= 40) {
						.@wz_skills$[.@wz_count] = "WZ_METEOR";
						.@wz_count++;
					}

					if (.@wz_count > 0) {
						.@chosen_skill$ = .@wz_skills$[@AUTO_SKILL_ROT % .@wz_count];
						unitskillusepos getcharid(3), .@chosen_skill$, getskilllv(.@chosen_skill$), .@mx, .@my;
						.@step_delay = 1300;
						addtimer .@step_delay, "AutoPlayerSystem::OnStepTimer";
						end;
					} else if (getskilllv("HW_NAPALMVULCAN") > 0 && Sp >= 50) {
						unitskilluseid getcharid(3), "HW_NAPALMVULCAN", getskilllv("HW_NAPALMVULCAN"), @AUTO_CURRENT_TARGET, 0, 0, 0, 0;
						.@step_delay = 600;
						addtimer .@step_delay, "AutoPlayerSystem::OnStepTimer";
						end;
					}
				}
			} else if (getskilllv("WZ_HEAVENDRIVE") > 0 && Sp >= 28 && .@is_wz && ((.@now - @AUTO_LAST_HEAVENDRIVE) >= 2 || getskilllv("MG_FIREBALL") <= 0)) {
				// Furia da Terra quando nao estiver em cooldown
				@AUTO_LAST_HEAVENDRIVE = .@now;
				unitskillusepos getcharid(3), "WZ_HEAVENDRIVE", getskilllv("WZ_HEAVENDRIVE"), .@mx, .@my;
				.@step_delay = 1200;
				addtimer .@step_delay, "AutoPlayerSystem::OnStepTimer";
				end;
			} else if (getskilllv("MG_FIREBALL") > 0 && Sp >= 15 && (.@is_wz || Class == Job_Mage || Class == Job_Mage_High)) {
				// Bruxo/Mago usa Bola de Fogo
				unitskilluseid getcharid(3), "MG_FIREBALL", getskilllv("MG_FIREBALL"), @AUTO_CURRENT_TARGET, 0, 0, 0, 0;
				.@step_delay = 700;
				addtimer .@step_delay, "AutoPlayerSystem::OnStepTimer";
				end;
			} else {
				if (getskilllv("MG_SOULSTRIKE") > 0 && Sp >= 15) {
					unitskilluseid getcharid(3), "MG_SOULSTRIKE", getskilllv("MG_SOULSTRIKE"), @AUTO_CURRENT_TARGET, 0, 0, 0, 0;
					.@step_delay = 600;
					addtimer .@step_delay, "AutoPlayerSystem::OnStepTimer";
					end;
				} else if (getskilllv("MG_FIREBOLT") > 0 && Sp >= 12) {
					unitskilluseid getcharid(3), "MG_FIREBOLT", getskilllv("MG_FIREBOLT"), @AUTO_CURRENT_TARGET, 0, 0, 0, 0;
					.@step_delay = 900;
					addtimer .@step_delay, "AutoPlayerSystem::OnStepTimer";
					end;
				} else if (getskilllv("MG_COLDBOLT") > 0 && Sp >= 12) {
					unitskilluseid getcharid(3), "MG_COLDBOLT", getskilllv("MG_COLDBOLT"), @AUTO_CURRENT_TARGET, 0, 0, 0, 0;
					.@step_delay = 900;
					addtimer .@step_delay, "AutoPlayerSystem::OnStepTimer";
					end;
				} else if (getskilllv("MG_LIGHTNINGBOLT") > 0 && Sp >= 12) {
					unitskilluseid getcharid(3), "MG_LIGHTNINGBOLT", getskilllv("MG_LIGHTNINGBOLT"), @AUTO_CURRENT_TARGET, 0, 0, 0, 0;
					.@step_delay = 900;
					addtimer .@step_delay, "AutoPlayerSystem::OnStepTimer";
					end;
				}
			}
	}
	// 3. ARQUEIRO E EVOLUCOES (Inclui Ranger, Menestrel, Cigana, Atirador de Elite, Bardo, etc.)
	else if (BaseClass == Job_Archer || Class == Job_Archer || Class == Job_Archer_High || BaseJob == Job_Archer || BaseJob == Job_Archer_High || Class == Job_Hunter || Class == Job_Sniper || Class == Job_Ranger || Class == Job_Ranger_T || Class == 4062 || Class == 4056 || Class == 4012 || Class == Job_Bard || Class == Job_Clown || Class == Job_Minstrel || Class == Job_Minstrel_T || Class == 4075 || Class == 4068 || Class == 4019 || Class == Job_Dancer || Class == Job_Gypsy || Class == Job_Wanderer || Class == Job_Wanderer_T || Class == 4076 || Class == 4069 || Class == 4020 || BaseJob == Job_Hunter || BaseJob == Job_Sniper || BaseJob == Job_Ranger || BaseJob == Job_Bard || BaseJob == Job_Clown || BaseJob == Job_Minstrel || BaseJob == Job_Dancer || BaseJob == Job_Gypsy || BaseJob == Job_Wanderer) {
		if (getskilllv("HT_BLITZBEAT") > 0 && !checkfalcon()) {
			if (getskilllv("HT_FALCON") <= 0) {
				skill "HT_FALCON", 1, 0;
			}
			setfalcon 1;
		}
		.@w_id = getequipid(EQI_HAND_R);
		.@w_type = (.@w_id > 0 ? getiteminfo(.@w_id, ITEMINFO_SUBTYPE) : 0);

		// Bardo / Menestrel: Mantem Banquete de Njord ativo apenas se for Bardo e estiver de instrumento (dura 180s)
		if ((Class == Job_Bard || Class == Job_Clown || Class == Job_Minstrel || Class == Job_Minstrel_T || Class == Job_Troubadour || Class == 4259 || Class == 4075) && .@w_type == W_MUSICAL) {
			if (getskilllv("BD_RICHMANKIM") > 0 && Sp >= (56 + getskilllv("BD_RICHMANKIM") * 6) && !getstatus(SC_RICHMANKIM) && .@now >= @BUFF_BD_RICHMANKIM + 180) {

		} else {
			@AUTO_ROGUE_STEP = 0;
			if (getskilllv("TF_POISON") > 0 && Sp >= 12) {
				unitskilluseid getcharid(3), "TF_POISON", getskilllv("TF_POISON"), @AUTO_CURRENT_TARGET, 0, 0, 0, 0;
				.@step_delay = 450;
			} else if (getskilllv("TF_SPRINKLESAND") > 0 && Sp >= 9) {
				unitskilluseid getcharid(3), "TF_SPRINKLESAND", getskilllv("TF_SPRINKLESAND"), @AUTO_CURRENT_TARGET, 0, 0, 0, 0;
				.@step_delay = 450;
			} else if (getskilllv("TF_DOUBLE") > 0) {
				.@step_delay = 400;
			}
		}
	}
	// 5. NOVICO E EVOLUCOES (Inclui Arcebispo, Shura, Sumo Sacerdote, Mestre, etc.)
	else if (BaseClass == Job_Acolyte || Class == Job_Acolyte || Class == Job_Acolyte_High || BaseJob == Job_Acolyte || BaseJob == Job_Acolyte_High || Class == Job_Priest || Class == Job_High_Priest || Class == Job_Arch_Bishop || Class == Job_Arch_Bishop_T || Class == 4063 || Class == 4057 || Class == 4010 || Class == Job_Monk || Class == Job_Champion || Class == Job_Sura || Class == Job_Sura_T || Class == 4077 || Class == 4070 || Class == 4016 || Class == Job_Inquisitor || BaseJob == Job_Priest || BaseJob == Job_High_Priest || BaseJob == Job_Arch_Bishop || BaseJob == Job_Monk || BaseJob == Job_Champion || BaseJob == Job_Sura || BaseJob == Job_Inquisitor) {
		.@is_ab_tree = (Class == Job_Priest || Class == Job_High_Priest || Class == Job_Arch_Bishop || Class == Job_Arch_Bishop_T || Class == 4063 || Class == 4057 || Class == 4010 || Class == Job_Cardinal || BaseJob == Job_Priest || BaseJob == Job_High_Priest || BaseJob == Job_Arch_Bishop || BaseJob == Job_Cardinal);
		.@sk_p$ = AutoSkillPriority$;

		// Prioridade configurada de habilidades ofensivas para Arcebispo / Sacerdote
		if (.@sk_p$ != "") {
			if (.@sk_p$ == "AB_ADORAMUS") {
				if (getskilllv("AB_ADORAMUS") > 0 && Sp >= 40 && .@now >= @CD_AB_ADORAMUS) {
					@CD_AB_ADORAMUS = .@now + 2;
					unitskilluseid getcharid(3), "AB_ADORAMUS", getskilllv("AB_ADORAMUS"), @AUTO_CURRENT_TARGET, 0, 0, 0, 0;
					.@step_delay = 800; addtimer .@step_delay, "AutoPlayerSystem::OnStepTimer"; end;
				} else if (getskilllv("AB_JUDEX") > 0 && Sp >= 20) {
					// Fallback continuo enquanto Adoramus estiver em cooldown de 2s ou sem SP
					unitskilluseid getcharid(3), "AB_JUDEX", getskilllv("AB_JUDEX"), @AUTO_CURRENT_TARGET, 0, 0, 0, 0;
					.@step_delay = 600; addtimer .@step_delay, "AutoPlayerSystem::OnStepTimer"; end;
				} else if (getskilllv("PR_MAGNUS") > 0 && Sp >= 40) {
					unitskillusepos getcharid(3), "PR_MAGNUS", getskilllv("PR_MAGNUS"), .@mx, .@my;
					.@step_delay = 1200; addtimer .@step_delay, "AutoPlayerSystem::OnStepTimer"; end;
				} else if (getskilllv("AL_HOLYLIGHT") > 0 && Sp >= 15) {
					unitskilluseid getcharid(3), "AL_HOLYLIGHT", getskilllv("AL_HOLYLIGHT"), @AUTO_CURRENT_TARGET, 0, 0, 0, 0;
					.@step_delay = 550; addtimer .@step_delay, "AutoPlayerSystem::OnStepTimer"; end;
				}
			} else if (.@sk_p$ == "AB_JUDEX" && getskilllv("AB_JUDEX") > 0 && Sp >= 20) {
				unitskilluseid getcharid(3), "AB_JUDEX", getskilllv("AB_JUDEX"), @AUTO_CURRENT_TARGET, 0, 0, 0, 0;
				.@step_delay = 600; addtimer .@step_delay, "AutoPlayerSystem::OnStepTimer"; end;
			} else if (.@sk_p$ == "PR_MAGNUS" && getskilllv("PR_MAGNUS") > 0 && Sp >= 40) {
				unitskillusepos getcharid(3), "PR_MAGNUS", getskilllv("PR_MAGNUS"), .@mx, .@my;
				.@step_delay = 1200; addtimer .@step_delay, "AutoPlayerSystem::OnStepTimer"; end;
			} else if (.@sk_p$ == "AL_HOLYLIGHT" && getskilllv("AL_HOLYLIGHT") > 0 && Sp >= 15) {
				unitskilluseid getcharid(3), "AL_HOLYLIGHT", getskilllv("AL_HOLYLIGHT"), @AUTO_CURRENT_TARGET, 0, 0, 0, 0;
				.@step_delay = 550; addtimer .@step_delay, "AutoPlayerSystem::OnStepTimer"; end;
			}
		}

		// Rotacao Padrao de Arcebispo / Sacerdote (quando nao definiu prioridade especifica):
		// Padrao: prioriza Adoramus sempre que disponivel; se em cooldown ou sem Adoramus, usa Judex / Magnus / Luz Divina
		if (.@is_ab_tree && (getskilllv("AB_ADORAMUS") > 0 || getskilllv("AB_JUDEX") > 0 || getskilllv("PR_MAGNUS") > 0 || getskilllv("AL_HOLYLIGHT") > 0)) {
			if (getskilllv("AB_ADORAMUS") > 0 && Sp >= 40 && .@now >= @CD_AB_ADORAMUS) {
				@CD_AB_ADORAMUS = .@now + 2;
				unitskilluseid getcharid(3), "AB_ADORAMUS", getskilllv("AB_ADORAMUS"), @AUTO_CURRENT_TARGET, 0, 0, 0, 0;
				.@step_delay = 800;
				addtimer .@step_delay, "AutoPlayerSystem::OnStepTimer";
				end;
			} else if (getskilllv("AB_JUDEX") > 0 && Sp >= 20) {
				unitskilluseid getcharid(3), "AB_JUDEX", getskilllv("AB_JUDEX"), @AUTO_CURRENT_TARGET, 0, 0, 0, 0;
				.@step_delay = 600;
				addtimer .@step_delay, "AutoPlayerSystem::OnStepTimer";
				end;
			} else if (getskilllv("PR_MAGNUS") > 0 && Sp >= 40 && (@AUTO_SKILL_ROT % 2 == 0)) {
				unitskillusepos getcharid(3), "PR_MAGNUS", getskilllv("PR_MAGNUS"), .@mx, .@my;
				.@step_delay = 1200;
				addtimer .@step_delay, "AutoPlayerSystem::OnStepTimer";
				end;
			} else if (getskilllv("AL_HOLYLIGHT") > 0 && Sp >= 15) {
				unitskilluseid getcharid(3), "AL_HOLYLIGHT", getskilllv("AL_HOLYLIGHT"), @AUTO_CURRENT_TARGET, 0, 0, 0, 0;
				.@step_delay = 550;
				addtimer .@step_delay, "AutoPlayerSystem::OnStepTimer";
				end;
			}
		} else {
			// ==============================================================
			// Shura / Monge / Mestre / Inquisidor:
			// ==============================================================
			// 1. Recuperacao de Esferas com Dragao Ascendente (Rising Dragon):
			// Se as esferas estiverem baixas (< 3) e possuir Dragao Ascendente:
			if (getskilllv("SR_RAISINGDRAGON") > 0 && Sp >= 120 && countspiritball() < 3 && .@now >= @CD_SR_RAISINGDRAGON) {
				@CD_SR_RAISINGDRAGON = .@now + 2;
				unitskilluseid getcharid(3), "SR_RAISINGDRAGON", getskilllv("SR_RAISINGDRAGON"), getcharid(3), 0, 0, 0, 0;
				.@step_delay = 400;
				addtimer .@step_delay, "AutoPlayerSystem::OnStepTimer";
				end;
			} else if (getskilllv("SR_RAISINGDRAGON") <= 0 && countspiritball() <= 0) {
				if (getskilllv("CH_SOULCOLLECT") > 0 && Sp >= 20) {
					unitskilluseid getcharid(3), "CH_SOULCOLLECT", getskilllv("CH_SOULCOLLECT"), getcharid(3), 0, 0, 0, 0;
					.@step_delay = 450;
					addtimer .@step_delay, "AutoPlayerSystem::OnStepTimer";
					end;
				} else if (getskilllv("MO_CALLSPIRITS") > 0 && Sp >= 8) {
					unitskilluseid getcharid(3), "MO_CALLSPIRITS", getskilllv("MO_CALLSPIRITS"), getcharid(3), 0, 0, 0, 0;
					.@step_delay = 400;
					addtimer .@step_delay, "AutoPlayerSystem::OnStepTimer";
					end;
				}
			}

			// Variaveis auxiliares de disponibilidade:
			.@can_tiger = (getskilllv("SR_TIGERCANNON") > 0 && Sp >= (30 + getskilllv("SR_TIGERCANNON") * 5) && Hp > 500 && (getstatus(SC_EXPLOSIONSPIRITS) || getstatus(SC_RAISINGDRAGON)) && .@dist <= 3 && .@now >= @CD_SR_TIGERCANNON);
			.@can_lion = (getskilllv("SR_HOWLINGOFLION") > 0 && Sp >= 70 && countspiritball() >= 3 && .@dist <= 4 && .@now >= @CD_SR_HOWLINGOFLION);
			.@can_rampage = (getskilllv("SR_RAMPAGEBLASTER") > 0 && Sp >= 100 && countspiritball() >= 1 && (getstatus(SC_EXPLOSIONSPIRITS) || getstatus(SC_RAISINGDRAGON)) && .@dist <= 3 && .@now >= @CD_SR_RAMPAGEBLASTER);
			.@can_skynet = (getskilllv("SR_SKYNETBLOW") > 0 && Sp >= (10 + getskilllv("SR_SKYNETBLOW") * 2) && .@dist <= 2);
			.@can_knuckle = (getskilllv("SR_KNUCKLEARROW") > 0 && Sp >= 20 && .@dist <= 7);
			.@can_gate = (getskilllv("SR_GATEOFHELL") > 0 && Sp >= 50 && countspiritball() >= 2 && (getstatus(SC_EXPLOSIONSPIRITS) || getstatus(SC_RAISINGDRAGON)) && .@dist <= 7 && .@now >= @CD_SR_GATEOFHELL);
			.@can_finger = (getskilllv("MO_FINGEROFFENSIVE") > 0 && countspiritball() > 0 && Sp >= (8 + getskilllv("MO_FINGEROFFENSIVE") * 4) && .@dist <= 8);

			.@skill_executed = 0;

			// ==============================================================
			// PRIORIDADE ESCOLHIDA EM @auto setup:
			// ==============================================================
			if ((AutoSkillPriority$ == "SR_TIGERCANNON" || #AutoSkillPriority == 1) && .@can_tiger) {
				@CD_SR_TIGERCANNON = .@now + 2;
				unitskilluseid getcharid(3), "SR_TIGERCANNON", getskilllv("SR_TIGERCANNON"), getcharid(3), 0, 0, 0, 0;
				.@step_delay = 550;
				.@skill_executed = 1;
			} else if ((AutoSkillPriority$ == "SR_HOWLINGOFLION" || #AutoSkillPriority == 2) && .@can_lion) {
				@CD_SR_HOWLINGOFLION = .@now + 10;
				unitskilluseid getcharid(3), "SR_HOWLINGOFLION", getskilllv("SR_HOWLINGOFLION"), getcharid(3), 0, 0, 0, 0;
				.@step_delay = 600;
				.@skill_executed = 1;
			} else if ((AutoSkillPriority$ == "SR_RAMPAGEBLASTER" || #AutoSkillPriority == 3) && .@can_rampage) {
				@CD_SR_RAMPAGEBLASTER = .@now + 10;
				unitskilluseid getcharid(3), "SR_RAMPAGEBLASTER", getskilllv("SR_RAMPAGEBLASTER"), getcharid(3), 0, 0, 0, 0;
				.@step_delay = 600;
				.@skill_executed = 1;
			} else if ((AutoSkillPriority$ == "SR_SKYNETBLOW" || #AutoSkillPriority == 4) && .@can_skynet) {
				unitskilluseid getcharid(3), "SR_SKYNETBLOW", getskilllv("SR_SKYNETBLOW"), getcharid(3), 0, 0, 0, 0;
				.@step_delay = 450;
				.@skill_executed = 1;
			} else if ((AutoSkillPriority$ == "SR_KNUCKLEARROW" || #AutoSkillPriority == 5) && .@can_knuckle) {
				unitskilluseid getcharid(3), "SR_KNUCKLEARROW", getskilllv("SR_KNUCKLEARROW"), @AUTO_CURRENT_TARGET, 0, 0, 0, 0;
				.@step_delay = 550;
				.@skill_executed = 1;
			} else if ((AutoSkillPriority$ == "SR_GATEOFHELL" || #AutoSkillPriority == 6) && .@can_gate) {
				@CD_SR_GATEOFHELL = .@now + 5;
				unitskilluseid getcharid(3), "SR_GATEOFHELL", getskilllv("SR_GATEOFHELL"), @AUTO_CURRENT_TARGET, 0, 0, 0, 0;
				.@step_delay = 650;
				.@skill_executed = 1;
			} else if ((AutoSkillPriority$ == "MO_FINGEROFFENSIVE" || #AutoSkillPriority == 7) && .@can_finger) {
				.@shoot_lv = getskilllv("MO_FINGEROFFENSIVE");
				if (.@shoot_lv > 5) .@shoot_lv = 5;
				if (.@shoot_lv < 1) .@shoot_lv = 1;
				unitskilluseid getcharid(3), "MO_FINGEROFFENSIVE", .@shoot_lv, @AUTO_CURRENT_TARGET, 0, 0, 0, 0;
				.@step_delay = 700;
				.@skill_executed = 1;
			}

			// ==============================================================
			// MODO AUTOMATICO OU FALLBACK QUANDO A PRIORITARIA ESTA EM COOLDOWN:
			// "qdo uma skill n estiver disponivel por cooldow ou por outro motivo usar as que estao disponivel"
			// 1. Garra do Tigre se estiver nivel maximo (10)
			// 2. Rugido do Leao se estiver nivel maximo (5)
			// 3. Explosao Espiritual se estiver nivel maximo (5)
			// 4. Soco Furacao se nao tiver as outras skills 3rd (ou durante cooldown)
			// 5. Demais habilidades 3rd aprendidas / Heranca de Mestre e Monge
			// ==============================================================
			if (!.@skill_executed) {
				if (getskilllv("SR_TIGERCANNON") >= 10 && .@can_tiger) {
					@CD_SR_TIGERCANNON = .@now + 2;
					unitskilluseid getcharid(3), "SR_TIGERCANNON", getskilllv("SR_TIGERCANNON"), getcharid(3), 0, 0, 0, 0;
					.@step_delay = 550;
					.@skill_executed = 1;
				} else if (getskilllv("SR_HOWLINGOFLION") >= 5 && .@can_lion) {
					@CD_SR_HOWLINGOFLION = .@now + 10;
					unitskilluseid getcharid(3), "SR_HOWLINGOFLION", getskilllv("SR_HOWLINGOFLION"), getcharid(3), 0, 0, 0, 0;
					.@step_delay = 600;
					.@skill_executed = 1;
				} else if (getskilllv("SR_RAMPAGEBLASTER") >= 5 && .@can_rampage) {
					@CD_SR_RAMPAGEBLASTER = .@now + 10;
					unitskilluseid getcharid(3), "SR_RAMPAGEBLASTER", getskilllv("SR_RAMPAGEBLASTER"), getcharid(3), 0, 0, 0, 0;
					.@step_delay = 600;
					.@skill_executed = 1;
				} else if (.@can_skynet) {
					// Soco Furacao: usado se nao tiver as outras 3rd ou enquanto estiverem em cooldown!
					unitskilluseid getcharid(3), "SR_SKYNETBLOW", getskilllv("SR_SKYNETBLOW"), getcharid(3), 0, 0, 0, 0;
					.@step_delay = 450;
					.@skill_executed = 1;
				} else if (.@can_tiger) {
					@CD_SR_TIGERCANNON = .@now + 2;
					unitskilluseid getcharid(3), "SR_TIGERCANNON", getskilllv("SR_TIGERCANNON"), getcharid(3), 0, 0, 0, 0;
					.@step_delay = 550;
					.@skill_executed = 1;
				} else if (.@can_lion) {
					@CD_SR_HOWLINGOFLION = .@now + 10;
					unitskilluseid getcharid(3), "SR_HOWLINGOFLION", getskilllv("SR_HOWLINGOFLION"), getcharid(3), 0, 0, 0, 0;
					.@step_delay = 600;
					.@skill_executed = 1;
				} else if (.@can_rampage) {
					@CD_SR_RAMPAGEBLASTER = .@now + 10;
					unitskilluseid getcharid(3), "SR_RAMPAGEBLASTER", getskilllv("SR_RAMPAGEBLASTER"), getcharid(3), 0, 0, 0, 0;
					.@step_delay = 600;
					.@skill_executed = 1;
				} else if (.@can_knuckle) {
					unitskilluseid getcharid(3), "SR_KNUCKLEARROW", getskilllv("SR_KNUCKLEARROW"), @AUTO_CURRENT_TARGET, 0, 0, 0, 0;
					.@step_delay = 550;
					.@skill_executed = 1;
				} else if (.@can_gate) {
					@CD_SR_GATEOFHELL = .@now + 5;
					unitskilluseid getcharid(3), "SR_GATEOFHELL", getskilllv("SR_GATEOFHELL"), @AUTO_CURRENT_TARGET, 0, 0, 0, 0;
					.@step_delay = 650;
					.@skill_executed = 1;
				} else if (.@can_finger) {
					.@shoot_lv = getskilllv("MO_FINGEROFFENSIVE");
					if (.@shoot_lv > 5) .@shoot_lv = 5;
					if (.@shoot_lv < 1) .@shoot_lv = 1;
					unitskilluseid getcharid(3), "MO_FINGEROFFENSIVE", .@shoot_lv, @AUTO_CURRENT_TARGET, 0, 0, 0, 0;
					.@step_delay = 700;
					.@skill_executed = 1;
				} else if (getskilllv("AL_HOLYLIGHT") > 0 && Sp >= 15 && getskilllv("MO_FINGEROFFENSIVE") <= 0) {
					unitskilluseid getcharid(3), "AL_HOLYLIGHT", getskilllv("AL_HOLYLIGHT"), @AUTO_CURRENT_TARGET, 0, 0, 0, 0;
					.@step_delay = 550;
					.@skill_executed = 1;
				} else if (getskilllv("MO_TRIPLEATTACK") > 0) {
					.@step_delay = 400;
				}
			}
		}
	}
	// 6. MERCADOR E EVOLUCOES (Inclui Mecanico, Bioquimico, Mestre-Ferreiro, Criador, etc.)
	else if (BaseClass == Job_Merchant || Class == Job_Merchant || Class == Job_Merchant_High || BaseJob == Job_Merchant || BaseJob == Job_Merchant_High || Class == Job_Blacksmith || Class == Job_Whitesmith || Class == Job_Mechanic || Class == Job_Mechanic_T || Class == 4064 || Class == 4058 || Class == 4070 || Class == 4071 || Class == 4011 || Class == Job_Alchemist || Class == Job_Creator || Class == Job_Genetic || Class == Job_Genetic_T || Class == 4078 || Class == 4072 || Class == 4018 || BaseJob == Job_Blacksmith || BaseJob == Job_Whitesmith || BaseJob == Job_Mechanic || BaseJob == Job_Alchemist || BaseJob == Job_Creator || BaseJob == Job_Genetic || Class == Job_Meister || BaseJob == Job_Meister) {
		// Arremessar Pocao Branca Nvl 4 no Homunculo se HP < 50% (delay 5s)
		if (getskilllv("AM_POTIONPITCHER") >= 4 && Sp >= 1 && countitem(504) > 0 && .@now >= @LAST_HOMUN_PITCHER_TICK + 5) {
			.@hom_gid = gethominfo(7);
			if (.@hom_gid > 0 && unitexists(.@hom_gid)) {
				deletearray .@h_data[0], 30;
				getunitdata .@hom_gid, .@h_data;
				if (.@h_data[UHOM_MAXHP] > 0 && (.@h_data[UHOM_HP] * 100 / .@h_data[UHOM_MAXHP]) < 50) {
					@LAST_HOMUN_PITCHER_TICK = .@now;
					unitskilluseid getcharid(3), "AM_POTIONPITCHER", 4, .@hom_gid, 0, 0, 0, 1;
					.@step_delay = 550;
					addtimer .@step_delay, "AutoPlayerSystem::OnStepTimer";
					end;
				}
			}
		}

		// Garante carrinho aberto caso tenha a habilidade
		if (!checkcart() && getskilllv("MC_PUSHCART") > 0) {
			setcart;
		}

		// Buffs da 1ª e 2ª Classe / Trans / 3ª (Mercador, Ferreiro, Mestre-Ferreiro, Mecanico, etc.)
		if (getskilllv("MC_LOUD") > 0 && Sp >= 8 && !getstatus(SC_LOUD) && .@now >= @BUFF_MC_LOUD + 10) {
			@BUFF_MC_LOUD = .@now;
			unitskilluseid getcharid(3), "MC_LOUD", getskilllv("MC_LOUD"), getcharid(3), 0, 0, 0, 0;
			.@step_delay = 450;
			addtimer .@step_delay, "AutoPlayerSystem::OnStepTimer";
			end;
		}
		if (getskilllv("BS_ADRENALINE") > 0 && Sp >= 20 && !getstatus(SC_ADRENALINE) && .@now >= @BUFF_BS_ADRENALINE + 15) {
			@BUFF_BS_ADRENALINE = .@now;
			unitskilluseid getcharid(3), "BS_ADRENALINE", getskilllv("BS_ADRENALINE"), getcharid(3), 0, 0, 0, 0;
			.@step_delay = 450;
			addtimer .@step_delay, "AutoPlayerSystem::OnStepTimer";
			end;
		}
		if (getskilllv("BS_WEAPONPERFECT") > 0 && Sp >= 18 && !getstatus(SC_WEAPONPERFECTION) && .@now >= @BUFF_BS_WEAPONPERFECT + 10) {
			@BUFF_BS_WEAPONPERFECT = .@now;
			unitskilluseid getcharid(3), "BS_WEAPONPERFECT", getskilllv("BS_WEAPONPERFECT"), getcharid(3), 0, 0, 0, 0;
			.@step_delay = 450;
			addtimer .@step_delay, "AutoPlayerSystem::OnStepTimer";
			end;
		}
		if (getskilllv("WS_OVERTHRUSTMAX") > 0 && Sp >= 20 && Zeny >= 5000 && !getstatus(SC_MAXOVERTHRUST) && !getstatus(SC_OVERTHRUST) && .@now >= @BUFF_WS_OVERTHRUSTMAX + 15) {
			@BUFF_WS_OVERTHRUSTMAX = .@now;
			unitskilluseid getcharid(3), "WS_OVERTHRUSTMAX", getskilllv("WS_OVERTHRUSTMAX"), getcharid(3), 0, 0, 0, 0;
			.@step_delay = 450;
			addtimer .@step_delay, "AutoPlayerSystem::OnStepTimer";
			end;
		} else if (getskilllv("BS_OVERTHRUST") > 0 && Sp >= 18 && !getstatus(SC_OVERTHRUST) && !getstatus(SC_MAXOVERTHRUST) && .@now >= @BUFF_BS_OVERTHRUST + 15) {
			@BUFF_BS_OVERTHRUST = .@now;
			unitskilluseid getcharid(3), "BS_OVERTHRUST", getskilllv("BS_OVERTHRUST"), getcharid(3), 0, 0, 0, 0;
			.@step_delay = 450;
			addtimer .@step_delay, "AutoPlayerSystem::OnStepTimer";
			end;
		}
		if (getskilllv("BS_MAXIMIZE") > 0 && Sp >= 20 && !getstatus(SC_MAXIMIZEPOWER) && .@now >= @BUFF_WS_MAXIMIZE + 10) {
			@BUFF_WS_MAXIMIZE = .@now;
			unitskilluseid getcharid(3), "BS_MAXIMIZE", getskilllv("BS_MAXIMIZE"), getcharid(3), 0, 0, 0, 0;
			.@step_delay = 450;
			addtimer .@step_delay, "AutoPlayerSystem::OnStepTimer";
			end;
		}
		if (checkcart()) {
			if (getskilllv("WS_CARTBOOST") > 0 && Sp >= 20 && !getstatus(SC_CARTBOOST) && .@now >= @BUFF_WS_CARTBOOST + 15) {
				@BUFF_WS_CARTBOOST = .@now;
				unitskilluseid getcharid(3), "WS_CARTBOOST", getskilllv("WS_CARTBOOST"), getcharid(3), 0, 0, 0, 0;
				.@step_delay = 450;
				addtimer .@step_delay, "AutoPlayerSystem::OnStepTimer";
				end;
			}
			if (getskilllv("GN_CARTBOOST") > 0 && Sp >= 20 && !getstatus(SC_GN_CARTBOOST) && .@now >= @BUFF_GN_CARTBOOST + 15) {
				@BUFF_GN_CARTBOOST = .@now;
				unitskilluseid getcharid(3), "GN_CARTBOOST", getskilllv("GN_CARTBOOST"), getcharid(3), 0, 0, 0, 0;
				.@step_delay = 450;
				addtimer .@step_delay, "AutoPlayerSystem::OnStepTimer";
				end;
			}
		}
		if (checkmadogear()) {
			if ((getequipid(EQI_ACC_L) == 2800 || getequipid(EQI_ACC_R) == 2800) && getskilllv("NC_ACCELERATION") > 0 && Sp >= 20 && !getstatus(SC_ACCELERATION) && .@now >= @BUFF_NC_ACCELERATION + 15) {
				@BUFF_NC_ACCELERATION = .@now;
				unitskilluseid getcharid(3), "NC_ACCELERATION", getskilllv("NC_ACCELERATION"), getcharid(3), 0, 0, 0, 0;
				.@step_delay = 450;
				addtimer .@step_delay, "AutoPlayerSystem::OnStepTimer";
				end;
			}

				unitskilluseid getcharid(3), "SS_HITOUAKUMU", getskilllv("SS_HITOUAKUMU"), getcharid(3), 0, 0, 0, 0;

			}
		}

		if (getskilllv("KO_HUUMARANKA") > 0 && Sp >= 22 && .@dist <= 10) {

		} else if (getskilllv("NJ_HYOUSENSOU") > 0 && Sp >= 15 && .@dist <= 9) {
			unitskilluseid getcharid(3), "NJ_HYOUSENSOU", getskilllv("NJ_HYOUSENSOU"), @AUTO_CURRENT_TARGET, 0, 0, 0, 0;
			.@step_delay = 600; addtimer .@step_delay, "AutoPlayerSystem::OnStepTimer"; end;
		} else if (getskilllv("NJ_HUUJIN") > 0 && Sp >= 12 && .@dist <= 9) {

	}
	// 8. JUSTICEIRO / REBELLION
	else if (BaseClass == Job_Gunslinger || Class == Job_Gunslinger || Class == Job_Rebellion || Class == Job_Night_Watch) {

			} else if (.@sk_p$ == "RL_R_TRIP" && getskilllv("RL_R_TRIP") > 0 && Sp >= 30) {
				unitskilluseid getcharid(3), "RL_R_TRIP", getskilllv("RL_R_TRIP"), getcharid(3), 0, 0, 0, 0;
				.@step_delay = 700; addtimer .@step_delay, "AutoPlayerSystem::OnStepTimer"; end;
			} else if (.@sk_p$ == "GS_DESPERADO" && getskilllv("GS_DESPERADO") > 0 && Sp >= 30) {
				unitskilluseid getcharid(3), "GS_DESPERADO", getskilllv("GS_DESPERADO"), getcharid(3), 0, 0, 0, 0;
				.@step_delay = 800; addtimer .@step_delay, "AutoPlayerSystem::OnStepTimer"; end;
			} else if (.@sk_p$ == "GS_TRIPLEACTION" && getskilllv("GS_TRIPLEACTION") > 0 && Sp >= 15) {
				unitskilluseid getcharid(3), "GS_TRIPLEACTION", getskilllv("GS_TRIPLEACTION"), @AUTO_CURRENT_TARGET, 0, 0, 0, 0;
				.@step_delay = 500; addtimer .@step_delay, "AutoPlayerSystem::OnStepTimer"; end;
			}
		}

		if (getskilllv("RL_FIREDANCE") > 0 && Sp >= 20) {
			unitskilluseid getcharid(3), "RL_FIREDANCE", getskilllv("RL_FIREDANCE"), getcharid(3), 0, 0, 0, 0;
			.@step_delay = 500;
		} else if (getskilllv("RL_R_TRIP") > 0 && Sp >= 30) {
			unitskilluseid getcharid(3), "RL_R_TRIP", getskilllv("RL_R_TRIP"), getcharid(3), 0, 0, 0, 0;
			.@step_delay = 700;
		} else if (getskilllv("GS_DESPERADO") > 0 && Sp >= 30) {
			unitskilluseid getcharid(3), "GS_DESPERADO", getskilllv("GS_DESPERADO"), getcharid(3), 0, 0, 0, 0;
			.@step_delay = 800;
		} else if (getskilllv("GS_TRIPLEACTION") > 0 && Sp >= 15) {
			unitskilluseid getcharid(3), "GS_TRIPLEACTION", getskilllv("GS_TRIPLEACTION"), @AUTO_CURRENT_TARGET, 0, 0, 0, 0;
			.@step_delay = 500;
		}
	}
	// 9. DORAM (SUMMONER)

				.@step_delay = 500; addtimer .@step_delay, "AutoPlayerSystem::OnStepTimer"; end;
			} else if (.@sk_p$ == "SU_SV_STEMSPEAR" && getskilllv("SU_SV_STEMSPEAR") > 0 && Sp >= 15) {
				unitskilluseid getcharid(3), "SU_SV_STEMSPEAR", getskilllv("SU_SV_STEMSPEAR"), @AUTO_CURRENT_TARGET, 0, 0, 0, 0;
				.@step_delay = 600; addtimer .@step_delay, "AutoPlayerSystem::OnStepTimer"; end;
			} else if (.@sk_p$ == "SU_CN_METEOR" && getskilllv("SU_CN_METEOR") > 0 && Sp >= 30) {
				unitskillusepos getcharid(3), "SU_CN_METEOR", getskilllv("SU_CN_METEOR"), .@mx, .@my;
				.@step_delay = 900; addtimer .@step_delay, "AutoPlayerSystem::OnStepTimer"; end;
			}
		}

		if (getskilllv("SU_PICKYPECK") > 0 && Sp >= 10) {
			unitskilluseid getcharid(3), "SU_PICKYPECK", getskilllv("SU_PICKYPECK"), @AUTO_CURRENT_TARGET, 0, 0, 0, 0;
			.@step_delay = 500;
		} else if (getskilllv("SU_SV_STEMSPEAR") > 0 && Sp >= 15) {
			unitskilluseid getcharid(3), "SU_SV_STEMSPEAR", getskilllv("SU_SV_STEMSPEAR"), @AUTO_CURRENT_TARGET, 0, 0, 0, 0;
			.@step_delay = 600;
		} else if (getskilllv("SU_CN_METEOR") > 0 && Sp >= 30 && (@AUTO_SKILL_ROT % 2 == 0)) {
			unitskillusepos getcharid(3), "SU_CN_METEOR", getskilllv("SU_CN_METEOR"), .@mx, .@my;
			.@step_delay = 900;
		}
	}
	// 10. TAEKWON E EVOLUCOES
	else if (BaseClass == Job_Taekwon || Class == Job_Taekwon || Class == Job_Star_Gladiator || Class == Job_Soul_Linker || Class == Job_Star_Emperor || Class == Job_Soul_Reaper) {
		.@sk_p$ = AutoSkillPriority$;
		if (.@sk_p$ != "") {
			if (.@sk_p$ == "TK_DOWNKICK" && getskilllv("TK_DOWNKICK") > 0 && Sp >= 1) {
				unitskilluseid getcharid(3), "TK_DOWNKICK", getskilllv("TK_DOWNKICK"), @AUTO_CURRENT_TARGET, 0, 0, 0, 0;
				.@step_delay = 450; addtimer .@step_delay, "AutoPlayerSystem::OnStepTimer"; end;
			} else if (.@sk_p$ == "TK_STORMKICK" && getskilllv("TK_STORMKICK") > 0 && Sp >= 1) {
				unitskilluseid getcharid(3), "TK_STORMKICK", getskilllv("TK_STORMKICK"), getcharid(3), 0, 0, 0, 0;
				.@step_delay = 450; addtimer .@step_delay, "AutoPlayerSystem::OnStepTimer"; end;
			} else if (.@sk_p$ == "TK_TURNKICK" && getskilllv("TK_TURNKICK") > 0 && Sp >= 1) {
				unitskilluseid getcharid(3), "TK_TURNKICK", getskilllv("TK_TURNKICK"), @AUTO_CURRENT_TARGET, 0, 0, 0, 0;
				.@step_delay = 450; addtimer .@step_delay, "AutoPlayerSystem::OnStepTimer"; end;
			} else if (.@sk_p$ == "TK_COUNTER" && getskilllv("TK_COUNTER") > 0 && Sp >= 1) {
				unitskilluseid getcharid(3), "TK_COUNTER", getskilllv("TK_COUNTER"), @AUTO_CURRENT_TARGET, 0, 0, 0, 0;
				.@step_delay = 450; addtimer .@step_delay, "AutoPlayerSystem::OnStepTimer"; end;
			}
		}
		.@step_delay = 400;
	}
	// 11. APRENDIZ E APRENDIZ T (NOVICE / NOVICE HIGH)
	else if (BaseClass == Job_Novice || Class == Job_Novice || Class == Job_Novice_High || BaseJob == Job_Novice || BaseJob == Job_Novice_High) {
		.@step_delay = 400;
	}

	addtimer .@step_delay, "AutoPlayerSystem::OnStepTimer";
	end;

// ======================================================================
// SUB-ROTINA: Calculo Dinamico do Alcance de Combate (.@atk_range)
// ======================================================================
// ======================================================================
// SISTEMA DE CONTORNO DE OBSTACULOS (PATHFINDING E DESVIO LATERAL)
// - 1. Caminho direto se <= 14 celulas
// - 2. Reta direta avancada se > 10 celulas
// - 3. Se houver obstaculo (a reta falhou): DESVIA contornando pelas laterais!
// ======================================================================
OnContourWalkTo:
	.@tgt_x = getarg(0);
	.@tgt_y = getarg(1);
	.@cur_x = 0;
	.@cur_y = 0;
	getmapxy .@m$, .@cur_x, .@cur_y;
	if (.@m$ == "") .@m$ = @AUTO_MAP$;
	if (.@m$ == "") .@m$ = strcharinfo(3);
	if (.@cur_x == 0 && .@cur_y == 0) {
		.@cur_x = @AUTO_STUCK_X;
		.@cur_y = @AUTO_STUCK_Y;
	}

	.@dist = distance(.@cur_x, .@cur_y, .@tgt_x, .@tgt_y);
	if (.@dist <= 0) return 1;

	// Se for Shura / Monge com Passo Etereo (Body Relocation) e recursos disponiveis:
	.@can_snap = (getskilllv("MO_BODYRELOCATION") > 0 && Sp >= 14 && (getstatus(SC_EXPLOSIONSPIRITS) || getstatus(SC_RAISINGDRAGON) || countspiritball() > 0));
	if (.@can_snap) {
		if (.@dist <= 14 && checkcell(.@m$, .@tgt_x, .@tgt_y, CELL_CHKPASS)) {
			unitstopwalk getcharid(3), USW_FORCE_STOP;
			unitskillusepos getcharid(3), "MO_BODYRELOCATION", 1, .@tgt_x, .@tgt_y;
			@AUTO_LAST_TX = .@tgt_x;
			@AUTO_LAST_TY = .@tgt_y;
			@AUTO_SNAP_USED = 1;
			return 1;
		}
		.@diff_x = .@tgt_x - .@cur_x;
		.@diff_y = .@tgt_y - .@cur_y;
		if (.@dist > 14) {
			.@fx = .@cur_x + ((.@diff_x * 14) / .@dist);
			.@fy = .@cur_y + ((.@diff_y * 14) / .@dist);
			if (checkcell(.@m$, .@fx, .@fy, CELL_CHKPASS)) {
				unitstopwalk getcharid(3), USW_FORCE_STOP;
				unitskillusepos getcharid(3), "MO_BODYRELOCATION", 1, .@fx, .@fy;
				@AUTO_LAST_TX = .@fx;
				@AUTO_LAST_TY = .@fy;
				@AUTO_SNAP_USED = 1;
				return 1;
			}
		}
	}

	// 1. Caminho direto: se <= 14 celulas e transitavel, anda direto ate o ponto
	// Em rAthena, unitwalk utiliza A* nativo (path_search) que contorna pequenos obstaculos
	if (.@dist <= 14 && checkcell(.@m$, .@tgt_x, .@tgt_y, CELL_CHKPASS)) {
		if (unitwalk(getcharid(3), .@tgt_x, .@tgt_y)) {
			@AUTO_LAST_TX = .@tgt_x;
			@AUTO_LAST_TY = .@tgt_y;
			return 1;
		}
	}

	// 2. Se o ponto estiver distante (> 10 celulas), tenta avancar NA RETA DIRETA (passos de 14 a 6 celulas)
	.@diff_x = .@tgt_x - .@cur_x;
	.@diff_y = .@tgt_y - .@cur_y;
	if (.@dist > 10) {
		for (.@step = 14; .@step >= 6; .@step -= 2) {
			.@fx = .@cur_x + ((.@diff_x * .@step) / .@dist);
			.@fy = .@cur_y + ((.@diff_y * .@step) / .@dist);
			if (checkcell(.@m$, .@fx, .@fy, CELL_CHKPASS)) {
				if (unitwalk(getcharid(3), .@fx, .@fy)) {
					@AUTO_LAST_TX = .@fx;
					@AUTO_LAST_TY = .@fy;
					return 1;
				}
			}
		}
	}

	// 3. SE ENCONTRAR OBSTACULO (o caminho reto falhou): DESVIA!
	// Testa desvios laterais contornando o obstaculo para a esquerda e para a direita
	.@dir_x = (.@diff_x > 0 ? 1 : (.@diff_x < 0 ? -1 : 0));
	.@dir_y = (.@diff_y > 0 ? 1 : (.@diff_y < 0 ? -1 : 0));
	.@pRx = .@dir_y;
	.@pRy = -(.@dir_x);

	.@step_d = (.@dist > 8 ? 8 : .@dist);
	if (.@step_d < 3) .@step_d = 3;
	.@base_x = .@cur_x + ((.@diff_x * .@step_d) / .@dist);
	.@base_y = .@cur_y + ((.@diff_y * .@step_d) / .@dist);

	// Offsets de desvio diagonal lateral (+3, -3, +4, -4, +5, -5, +2, -2)
	setarray .@lat_off[0], 3, -3, 4, -4, 5, -5, 2, -2;
	for (.@i = 0; .@i < 8; .@i++) {
		.@off = .@lat_off[.@i];
		// Desvio diagonal avancado (contorna a pedra e segue em frente)
		.@dx = .@base_x + (.@pRx * .@off);
		.@dy = .@base_y + (.@pRy * .@off);
		if (checkcell(.@m$, .@dx, .@dy, CELL_CHKPASS)) {
			if (unitwalk(getcharid(3), .@dx, .@dy)) {
				@AUTO_LAST_TX = .@dx;
				@AUTO_LAST_TY = .@dy;
				return 1;
			}
		}
	}

	return 0;

// ======================================================================
// CALCULO DINAMICO DO ALCANCE DE COMBATE IDEAL POR CLASSE E ARMA
// ======================================================================
// Reconhece a habilidade que o jogador usara e define o range ideal:
// - Habilidades de longa distancia (ex: Luz Divina): mantem distancia (8 celulas)
// - Turbilhao de Petalas: aproxima ate alcance seguro (5 celulas)
// - Ataques corpo-a-corpo / sem SP: aproxima ate alcance da arma (2 celulas)
// ======================================================================
OnGetCombatRange:
	.@w_id = getequipid(EQI_HAND_R);
	.@w_range = 1;
	if (.@w_id > 0) {
		.@w_range = getiteminfo(.@w_id, ITEMINFO_RANGE);
		if (.@w_range <= 0) .@w_range = 1;
	}
	.@range = .@w_range;

	.@cur_p$ = AutoSkillPriority$;
	if (.@cur_p$ == "" && #AutoSkillPriority > 0) {
		if (#AutoSkillPriority == 1) .@cur_p$ = "SR_TIGERCANNON";
		else if (#AutoSkillPriority == 2) .@cur_p$ = "SR_HOWLINGOFLION";
		else if (#AutoSkillPriority == 3) .@cur_p$ = "SR_RAMPAGEBLASTER";
		else if (#AutoSkillPriority == 4) .@cur_p$ = "SR_SKYNETBLOW";
		else if (#AutoSkillPriority == 5) .@cur_p$ = "SR_KNUCKLEARROW";
		else if (#AutoSkillPriority == 6) .@cur_p$ = "SR_GATEOFHELL";
		else if (#AutoSkillPriority == 7) .@cur_p$ = "MO_FINGEROFFENSIVE";
	}
	if (.@cur_p$ != "" && getskilllv(.@cur_p$) > 0) {
		if (.@cur_p$ == "SR_TIGERCANNON" || .@cur_p$ == "SR_SKYNETBLOW" || .@cur_p$ == "RK_IGNITIONBREAK" || .@cur_p$ == "RK_WINDCUTTER" || .@cur_p$ == "KN_BOWLINGBASH" || .@cur_p$ == "SM_BASH" || .@cur_p$ == "SM_MAGNUM" || .@cur_p$ == "CR_HOLYCROSS" || .@cur_p$ == "LG_RAYOFGENESIS" || .@cur_p$ == "GC_ROLLINGCUTTER" || .@cur_p$ == "AS_SONICBLOW" || .@cur_p$ == "RG_BACKSTAP" || .@cur_p$ == "RG_RAID" || .@cur_p$ == "TF_POISON" || .@cur_p$ == "TF_SPRINKLESAND" || .@cur_p$ == "GN_CART_TORNADO" || .@cur_p$ == "MC_CARTREVOLUTION" || .@cur_p$ == "MC_MAMMONITE" || .@cur_p$ == "WS_CARTTERMINATION" || .@cur_p$ == "GS_DESPERADO" || .@cur_p$ == "TK_DOWNKICK" || .@cur_p$ == "TK_STORMKICK" || .@cur_p$ == "TK_TURNKICK" || .@cur_p$ == "TK_COUNTER" || .@cur_p$ == "NC_AXETORNADO" || .@cur_p$ == "NC_POWERSWING" || .@cur_p$ == "NC_MAGMA_ERUPTION" || .@cur_p$ == "MT_AXE_STOMP" || .@cur_p$ == "MT_RUSH_QUAKE") {
			return (.@w_range > 0 && .@w_range < 2 ? .@w_range : 2);
		}
		if (.@cur_p$ == "GC_CROSSIMPACT" || .@cur_p$ == "SC_FATALMENACE" || .@cur_p$ == "ASC_METEORASSAULT" || .@cur_p$ == "LG_OVERBRAND" || .@cur_p$ == "PA_SHIELDCHAIN" || .@cur_p$ == "SR_HOWLINGOFLION" || .@cur_p$ == "SR_RAMPAGEBLASTER" || .@cur_p$ == "RL_FIREDANCE" || .@cur_p$ == "NC_PILEBUNKER") {
			return 3;
		}
		if (.@cur_p$ == "NC_FLAMELAUNCHER" || .@cur_p$ == "NC_COLDSLOWER") {
			return 5;
		}
		if (.@cur_p$ == "LK_SPIRALPIERCE" || .@cur_p$ == "SR_KNUCKLEARROW" || .@cur_p$ == "SR_GATEOFHELL" || .@cur_p$ == "GN_CARTCANNON" || .@cur_p$ == "NC_ARMSCANNON" || .@cur_p$ == "NC_AXEBOOMERANG" || .@cur_p$ == "NC_BOOSTKNUCKLE" || .@cur_p$ == "NC_VULCANARM" || .@cur_p$ == "LG_BANISHINGPOINT" || .@cur_p$ == "LG_CANNONSPEAR" || .@cur_p$ == "WL_EARTHSTRAIN" || .@cur_p$ == "KO_HUUMARANKA" || .@cur_p$ == "KO_BAKURETSU" || .@cur_p$ == "NJ_HUUMA" || .@cur_p$ == "NJ_KOUENKA" || .@cur_p$ == "RL_R_TRIP" || .@cur_p$ == "GS_TRIPLEACTION" || .@cur_p$ == "SU_PICKYPECK" || .@cur_p$ == "SU_SV_STEMSPEAR") {
			return 7;
		}
		return 8;
	}
	if (BaseClass == Job_Swordman || Class == Job_Swordman || Class == Job_Swordman_High || BaseJob == Job_Swordman || BaseJob == Job_Swordman_High || Class == Job_Knight || Class == Job_Lord_Knight || Class == Job_Rune_Knight || Class == Job_Rune_Knight_T || Class == 4060 || Class == 4054 || Class == 4008 || Class == Job_Crusader || Class == Job_Paladin || Class == Job_Royal_Guard || Class == Job_Royal_Guard_T || Class == 4073 || Class == 4066 || Class == 4015 || BaseJob == Job_Knight || BaseJob == Job_Crusader || BaseJob == Job_Lord_Knight || BaseJob == Job_Paladin || BaseJob == Job_Rune_Knight || BaseJob == Job_Royal_Guard) {
		if (getskilllv("RK_DRAGONBREATH_WATER") >= 10 && (checkriding() || checkdragon()) && Sp >= 30) {
			.@range = 7;
		} else if (getskilllv("RK_IGNITIONBREAK") > 0 || getskilllv("RK_WINDCUTTER") > 0) {
			.@range = (.@w_range > 0 && .@w_range < 2 ? .@w_range : 2);
		} else if (getskilllv("RK_DRAGONBREATH") > 0 && (checkriding() || checkdragon()) && Sp >= 30) {
			.@range = 7;
		} else if (getskilllv("RK_SONICWAVE") > 0 && Sp >= 35) {
			.@range = 7;
		} else if ((getskilllv("LG_BANISHINGPOINT") > 0 && Sp >= 20) || (getskilllv("LG_CANNONSPEAR") > 0 && Sp >= 30)) {
			.@range = 6;
		} else if (getskilllv("LK_SPIRALPIERCE") > 0 && Sp >= 20) {
			.@range = 5;
		} else if (getskilllv("PA_SHIELDCHAIN") > 0 && Sp >= (25 + getskilllv("PA_SHIELDCHAIN") * 3) && getequipid(EQI_HAND_L) > 0) {
			.@range = 7;
		} else {
			.@range = .@w_range;
		}
	}
	// MAGO E EVOLUCOES (Inclui Arcano 4061, Feiticeiro 4074, Arquimago 4009, Professor 4017, etc.)
	else if (BaseClass == Job_Mage || Class == Job_Mage || Class == Job_Mage_High || BaseJob == Job_Mage || BaseJob == Job_Mage_High || Class == Job_Wizard || Class == Job_High_Wizard || Class == Job_Warlock || Class == Job_Warlock_T || Class == 4061 || Class == Job_Sage || Class == Job_Professor || Class == Job_Sorcerer || Class == Job_Sorcerer_T || Class == 4074 || Class == 4067 || Class == 4055 || Class == 4009 || Class == 4017 || BaseJob == Job_Wizard || BaseJob == Job_Sage || BaseJob == Job_High_Wizard || BaseJob == Job_Professor || BaseJob == Job_Warlock || BaseJob == Job_Sorcerer) {
		if (getskilllv("WL_EARTHSTRAIN") > 0 && Sp >= 70) {

		} else {

	// ARQUEIRO E EVOLUCOES (Inclui Sentinela 4062, Menestrel 4075, Musa/Cigana 4076, Atirador de Elite 4012, etc.)
	else if (BaseClass == Job_Archer || Class == Job_Archer || Class == Job_Archer_High || BaseJob == Job_Archer || BaseJob == Job_Archer_High || Class == Job_Hunter || Class == Job_Sniper || Class == Job_Ranger || Class == Job_Ranger_T || Class == 4062 || Class == 4056 || Class == 4012 || Class == Job_Bard || Class == Job_Clown || Class == Job_Minstrel || Class == Job_Minstrel_T || Class == 4075 || Class == 4068 || Class == 4019 || Class == Job_Dancer || Class == Job_Gypsy || Class == Job_Wanderer || Class == Job_Wanderer_T || Class == 4076 || Class == 4069 || Class == 4020 || BaseJob == Job_Hunter || BaseJob == Job_Sniper || BaseJob == Job_Ranger || BaseJob == Job_Bard || BaseJob == Job_Clown || BaseJob == Job_Minstrel || BaseJob == Job_Dancer || BaseJob == Job_Gypsy || BaseJob == Job_Wanderer) {

	}
	// GATUNO E EVOLUCOES (Inclui Sicario 4065, Renegado 4079, Algoz 4013, Desordeiro 4021, etc.)

			.@range = .@w_range;
		}
	}
	// ACOLITO E EVOLUCOES (Inclui Arcebispo 4063, Shura 4077, Sumo Sacerdote 4010, Mestre 4016, etc.)
	else if (BaseClass == Job_Acolyte || Class == Job_Acolyte || Class == Job_Acolyte_High || BaseJob == Job_Acolyte || BaseJob == Job_Acolyte_High || Class == Job_Priest || Class == Job_High_Priest || Class == Job_Arch_Bishop || Class == Job_Arch_Bishop_T || Class == 4063 || Class == 4057 || Class == 4010 || Class == Job_Monk || Class == Job_Champion || Class == Job_Sura || Class == Job_Sura_T || Class == 4077 || Class == 4070 || Class == 4016 || Class == Job_Inquisitor || BaseJob == Job_Priest || BaseJob == Job_High_Priest || BaseJob == Job_Arch_Bishop || BaseJob == Job_Monk || BaseJob == Job_Champion || BaseJob == Job_Sura || BaseJob == Job_Inquisitor) {

		} else if (#AutoSkillPriority == 5 && getskilllv("SR_KNUCKLEARROW") > 0 && Sp >= 15) {

		} else if (#AutoSkillPriority == 6 && getskilllv("SR_GATEOFHELL") > 0 && Sp >= 50) {

		} else if (getskilllv("MO_FINGEROFFENSIVE") > 0 && Sp >= 20) {

	// MERCADOR E EVOLUCOES (Inclui Mecanico 4064, Bioquimico 4078, Mestre-Ferreiro 4011, Criador 4018, Meister, etc.)
	else if (BaseClass == Job_Merchant || Class == Job_Merchant || Class == Job_Merchant_High || BaseJob == Job_Merchant || BaseJob == Job_Merchant_High || Class == Job_Blacksmith || Class == Job_Whitesmith || Class == Job_Mechanic || Class == Job_Mechanic_T || Class == 4064 || Class == 4058 || Class == 4070 || Class == 4071 || Class == 4011 || Class == Job_Alchemist || Class == Job_Creator || Class == Job_Genetic || Class == Job_Genetic_T || Class == 4078 || Class == 4072 || Class == 4018 || BaseJob == Job_Blacksmith || BaseJob == Job_Whitesmith || BaseJob == Job_Mechanic || BaseJob == Job_Alchemist || BaseJob == Job_Creator || BaseJob == Job_Genetic || Class == Job_Meister || BaseJob == Job_Meister) {
		if (checkmadogear()) {
			// Mecanico no Robo (Mado Gear): mantem alcance a distancia do Canhao/Punho
			if ((getskilllv("NC_ARMSCANNON") > 0 || getskilllv("NC_BOOSTKNUCKLE") > 0 || getskilllv("NC_VULCANARM") > 0) && Sp >= 15) {
				.@range = 7;

			// Prioridade 2 Bioquimico: Esporo Explosivo
			.@range = 8;
		} else if (getskilllv("GN_CART_TORNADO") > 0 && Sp >= 30 && .@now >= @CD_GN_CART_TORNADO && (getskilllv("GN_CARTCANNON") < 5 || .@now < @CD_GN_CARTCANNON) && (getskilllv("GN_SPORE_EXPLOSION") <= 0 || .@now < @CD_GN_SPORE_EXPLOSION)) {
			// Prioridade 3 Bioquimico: Tornado de Carrinho (aproxima para alcance corpo-a-corpo)
			.@range = (.@w_range > 0 && .@w_range < 2 ? .@w_range : 2);
		} else if (.@cur_p$ == "NC_AXEBOOMERANG" && getskilllv("NC_AXEBOOMERANG") > 0 && Sp >= 20) {
			// Apenas se priorizou explicitamente Bumerangue de Machado mantem distancia
			.@range = 6;
		} else if (getskilllv("NC_AXETORNADO") > 0 || getskilllv("NC_POWERSWING") > 0 || getskilllv("NC_MAGMA_ERUPTION") > 0 || getskilllv("MT_AXE_STOMP") > 0 || getskilllv("MT_RUSH_QUAKE") > 0 || getskilllv("WS_CARTTERMINATION") > 0 || getskilllv("MC_CARTREVOLUTION") > 0 || getskilllv("MC_MAMMONITE") > 0) {
			// Mecanico Machadeiro / Ferreiro a pe: SEMPRE fecha para alcance corpo-a-corpo (2 celulas)!
			.@range = (.@w_range > 0 && .@w_range < 2 ? .@w_range : 2);
		} else if ((getskilllv("AM_ACIDTERROR") > 0 || getskilllv("CR_ACIDDEMONSTRATION") > 0) && Sp >= 15) {
			.@range = 8; // Terror Acido e Bomba Acida atacam a 8 celulas de distancia
		} else if (getskilllv("GN_SPORE_EXPLOSION") > 0 && Sp >= 35) {
			.@range = 8;
		} else if (getskilllv("GN_CARTCANNON") > 0 && Sp >= 30) {
			.@range = 7;
		} else {
			.@range = .@w_range;
		}
	}
	// NINJA E EVOLUCOES (Kagerou, Oboro, Shinkiro, Shiranui)
	else if (BaseClass == Job_Ninja || Class == Job_Ninja || Class == Job_Kagerou || Class == Job_Oboro || Class == Job_Shinkiro || Class == Job_Shiranui) {
		.@sk_p$ = AutoSkillPriority$;
		if (.@sk_p$ != "") {
			if (.@sk_p$ == "SS_KAGENOMAI" || .@sk_p$ == "SS_KAGEGISSEN" || .@sk_p$ == "SS_KUNAIKAITEN" || .@sk_p$ == "SS_KUNAIKUSSETSU" || .@sk_p$ == "SS_FUUMAKOUCHIKU" || .@sk_p$ == "SS_SEKIENHOU" || .@sk_p$ == "SS_RAIDENPOU" || .@sk_p$ == "SS_ANTENPOU" || .@sk_p$ == "SS_KAGEGARI" || .@sk_p$ == "SS_ANKOKURYUUAKUMU" || .@sk_p$ == "SS_KAGEAKUMU" || .@sk_p$ == "SS_HITOUAKUMU" || .@sk_p$ == "KO_JYUMONJIKIRI" || .@sk_p$ == "KO_BAKURETSU" || .@sk_p$ == "KO_HAPPOKUNAI" || .@sk_p$ == "NJ_HYOUSYOURAKU" || .@sk_p$ == "NJ_KAMAITACHI") {
				.@range = (.@w_range > 0 && .@w_range < 2 ? .@w_range : 2);
			} else {
				.@range = 6;
			}
		} else if (Class == Job_Shinkiro || Class == Job_Shiranui) {
			if (getskilllv("SS_KAGENOMAI") > 0 || getskilllv("SS_KAGEGISSEN") > 0 || getskilllv("SS_KUNAIKAITEN") > 0 || getskilllv("SS_KUNAIKUSSETSU") > 0 || getskilllv("SS_SEKIENHOU") > 0 || getskilllv("SS_RAIDENPOU") > 0 || getskilllv("SS_ANTENPOU") > 0 || getskilllv("SS_FUUMAKOUCHIKU") > 0 || getskilllv("SS_KAGEGARI") > 0 || getskilllv("SS_ANKOKURYUUAKUMU") > 0 || getskilllv("SS_KAGEAKUMU") > 0 || getskilllv("SS_HITOUAKUMU") > 0) {
				.@range = (.@w_range > 0 && .@w_range < 2 ? .@w_range : 2);
			} else if (getskilllv("SS_FUUMASHOUAKU") > 0 || getskilllv("SS_KUNAIWAIKYOKU") > 0 || getskilllv("SS_REIKETSUHOU") > 0 || getskilllv("SS_KINRYUUHOU") > 0 || getskilllv("SS_SHIMIRU") > 0) {
				.@range = 6;
			} else if (getskilllv("KO_JYUMONJIKIRI") > 0 || getskilllv("KO_BAKURETSU") > 0 || getskilllv("KO_HAPPOKUNAI") > 0 || getskilllv("NJ_HYOUSYOURAKU") > 0 || getskilllv("NJ_KAMAITACHI") > 0) {
				.@range = (.@w_range > 0 && .@w_range < 2 ? .@w_range : 2);
			} else if (getskilllv("KO_HUUMARANKA") > 0 || getskilllv("KO_KAIHOU") > 0 || getskilllv("NJ_KOUENKA") > 0 || getskilllv("NJ_HYOUSENSOU") > 0 || getskilllv("NJ_HUUJIN") > 0 || getskilllv("NJ_RAIGEKISAI") > 0 || getskilllv("NJ_BAKUENRYU") > 0 || getskilllv("NJ_HUUMA") > 0) {
				.@range = 6;
			} else {
				.@range = .@w_range;
			}
		} else {
			if (getskilllv("KO_JYUMONJIKIRI") > 0 || getskilllv("KO_BAKURETSU") > 0 || getskilllv("KO_HAPPOKUNAI") > 0 || getskilllv("NJ_HYOUSYOURAKU") > 0 || getskilllv("NJ_KAMAITACHI") > 0) {
				.@range = (.@w_range > 0 && .@w_range < 2 ? .@w_range : 2);
			} else if (getskilllv("KO_HUUMARANKA") > 0 || getskilllv("KO_KAIHOU") > 0 || getskilllv("NJ_KOUENKA") > 0 || getskilllv("NJ_HYOUSENSOU") > 0 || getskilllv("NJ_HUUJIN") > 0 || getskilllv("NJ_RAIGEKISAI") > 0 || getskilllv("NJ_BAKUENRYU") > 0 || getskilllv("NJ_HUUMA") > 0) {
				.@range = 6;
			} else {

	else if (BaseClass == Job_Gunslinger || Class == Job_Gunslinger || Class == Job_Rebellion) {

	else if (BaseClass == Job_Summoner || Class == Job_Summoner) {
		if (Sp >= 10) {

		} else {

		}
	}

	return .@range;

// ======================================================================
// SUB-ROTINA: Aplicacao Direta de Buffs (Substitui comando @buff)
// Aplica Bencao, Aumentar Agilidade, Magnificat, Gloria e Impositio
// diretamente no jogador sem depender de comandos de chat
// ======================================================================
OnApplyBaseBuffs:
	specialeffect2 EF_BLESSING;
	sc_start SC_BLESSING, 600000, 10;
	specialeffect2 EF_INCAGILITY;
	sc_start SC_INCREASEAGI, 600000, 10;
	sc_start SC_MAGNIFICAT, 600000, 5;
	sc_start SC_GLORIA, 600000, 5;
	sc_start SC_IMPOSITIO, 600000, 5;
	if (BaseClass == Job_Mage || Class == Job_Mage || Class == Job_Mage_High || BaseJob == Job_Mage || BaseJob == Job_Mage_High || Class == Job_Wizard || Class == Job_High_Wizard || Class == Job_Warlock || Class == Job_Warlock_T || Class == 4061 || Class == Job_Sage || Class == Job_Professor || Class == Job_Sorcerer || Class == Job_Sorcerer_T || Class == 4074) {
		specialeffect2 EF_ENERGYCOAT;
		sc_start SC_ENERGYCOAT, 600000, 1;
	}
	if (Class != Job_Bard && Class != Job_Clown && Class != Job_Minstrel && Class != Job_Minstrel_T && Class != Job_Troubadour && Class != 4259 &&
	    Class != Job_Dancer && Class != Job_Gypsy && Class != Job_Wanderer && Class != Job_Wanderer_T && Class != Job_Trouvere && Class != 4260) {
		if (getstatus(SC_POEMBRAGI)) sc_end SC_POEMBRAGI;
		if (getstatus(SC_RICHMANKIM)) sc_end SC_RICHMANKIM;
		if (getstatus(SC_ASSNCROS)) sc_end SC_ASSNCROS;
		if (getstatus(SC_APPLEIDUN)) sc_end SC_APPLEIDUN;
		if (getstatus(SC_WHISTLE)) sc_end SC_WHISTLE;
		if (getstatus(SC_RUSHWINDMILL)) sc_end SC_RUSHWINDMILL;
		if (getstatus(SC_SWINGDANCE)) sc_end SC_SWINGDANCE;
	}
	if (getgroupid() != 0) {
		sc_start SC_FOOD_STR_CASH, 600000, 10;
		sc_start SC_FOOD_AGI_CASH, 600000, 10;
		sc_start SC_FOOD_VIT_CASH, 600000, 10;
		sc_start SC_FOOD_INT_CASH, 600000, 10;
		sc_start SC_FOOD_DEX_CASH, 600000, 10;
		sc_start SC_FOOD_LUK_CASH, 600000, 10;
	}
	return;

// ======================================================================
// SUB-ROTINA: Reconhecimento e Uso de Habilidades de Suporte (Auto-Buffs)
// Reconhece habilidades aprendidas e as mantem ativas respeitando a
// duracao do buff ou delay minimo de 1 minuto (60 segundos).
// Retorna 1 se usou alguma habilidade (para aguardar delay), ou 0 se nao.
// ======================================================================
OnCheckSupportBuffs:
	.@now = gettimetick(2);

	// 1. ESPADACHIM E EVOLUCOES
	if (BaseClass == Job_Swordman || Class == Job_Swordman || Class == Job_Swordman_High || BaseJob == Job_Swordman || BaseJob == Job_Swordman_High) {
		if (getskilllv("LK_CONCENTRATION") > 0 && Sp >= 20 && !getstatus(SC_CONCENTRATION) && .@now >= @BUFF_LK_CONCENTRATION + 60) {
			@BUFF_LK_CONCENTRATION = .@now;
			unitskilluseid getcharid(3), "LK_CONCENTRATION", getskilllv("LK_CONCENTRATION"), getcharid(3), 0, 0, 0, 0;
			return 1;
		}
		if (getskilllv("LK_AURABLADE") > 0 && Sp >= 20 && !getstatus(SC_AURABLADE) && .@now >= @BUFF_LK_AURABLADE + 60) {
			@BUFF_LK_AURABLADE = .@now;
			unitskilluseid getcharid(3), "LK_AURABLADE", getskilllv("LK_AURABLADE"), getcharid(3), 0, 0, 0, 0;
			return 1;
		}
		if (getskilllv("KN_TWOHANDQUICKEN") > 0 && Sp >= 20 && !getstatus(SC_TWOHANDQUICKEN) && .@now >= @BUFF_KN_TWOHANDQUICKEN + 60) {
			@BUFF_KN_TWOHANDQUICKEN = .@now;
			unitskilluseid getcharid(3), "KN_TWOHANDQUICKEN", getskilllv("KN_TWOHANDQUICKEN"), getcharid(3), 0, 0, 0, 0;
			return 1;
		}
		if (getskilllv("SM_ENDURE") > 0 && Sp >= 10 && !getstatus(SC_ENDURE) && .@now >= @BUFF_SM_ENDURE + 60) {
			@BUFF_SM_ENDURE = .@now;
			unitskilluseid getcharid(3), "SM_ENDURE", getskilllv("SM_ENDURE"), getcharid(3), 0, 0, 0, 0;
			return 1;
		}
		if (getskilllv("RK_ENCHANTBLADE") > 0 && Sp >= 40 && !getstatus(SC_ENCHANTBLADE) && .@now >= @BUFF_RK_ENCHANTBLADE + 60) {
			@BUFF_RK_ENCHANTBLADE = .@now;
			unitskilluseid getcharid(3), "RK_ENCHANTBLADE", getskilllv("RK_ENCHANTBLADE"), getcharid(3), 0, 0, 0, 0;
			return 1;
		}
		if (getskilllv("CR_AUTOGUARD") > 0 && Sp >= 15 && !getstatus(SC_AUTOGUARD) && .@now >= @BUFF_CR_AUTOGUARD + 60) {
			@BUFF_CR_AUTOGUARD = .@now;
			unitskilluseid getcharid(3), "CR_AUTOGUARD", getskilllv("CR_AUTOGUARD"), getcharid(3), 0, 0, 0, 0;
			return 1;
		}
		if (getskilllv("CR_REFLECTSHIELD") > 0 && Sp >= 20 && !getstatus(SC_REFLECTSHIELD) && .@now >= @BUFF_CR_REFLECTSHIELD + 60) {
			@BUFF_CR_REFLECTSHIELD = .@now;
			unitskilluseid getcharid(3), "CR_REFLECTSHIELD", getskilllv("CR_REFLECTSHIELD"), getcharid(3), 0, 0, 0, 0;
			return 1;
		}
		if (getskilllv("CR_SPEARQUICKEN") > 0 && Sp >= 24 && !getstatus(SC_SPEARQUICKEN) && .@now >= @BUFF_CR_SPEARQUICKEN + 60) {
			@BUFF_CR_SPEARQUICKEN = .@now;
			unitskilluseid getcharid(3), "CR_SPEARQUICKEN", getskilllv("CR_SPEARQUICKEN"), getcharid(3), 0, 0, 0, 0;
			return 1;
		}
		if (getskilllv("LG_FORCEOFVANGUARD") > 0 && Sp >= 30 && !getstatus(SC_FORCEOFVANGUARD) && .@now >= @BUFF_LG_FORCEOFVANGUARD + 60) {
			@BUFF_LG_FORCEOFVANGUARD = .@now;
			unitskilluseid getcharid(3), "LG_FORCEOFVANGUARD", getskilllv("LG_FORCEOFVANGUARD"), getcharid(3), 0, 0, 0, 0;
			return 1;
		}
		if (getskilllv("LG_PRESTIGE") > 0 && Sp >= 40 && !getstatus(SC_PRESTIGE) && .@now >= @BUFF_LG_PRESTIGE + 60) {
			@BUFF_LG_PRESTIGE = .@now;
			unitskilluseid getcharid(3), "LG_PRESTIGE", getskilllv("LG_PRESTIGE"), getcharid(3), 0, 0, 0, 0;
			return 1;
		}
		if (getskilllv("LG_INSPIRATION") > 0 && Sp >= 80 && !getstatus(SC_INSPIRATION) && .@now >= @BUFF_LG_INSPIRATION + 60) {
			@BUFF_LG_INSPIRATION = .@now;
			unitskilluseid getcharid(3), "LG_INSPIRATION", getskilllv("LG_INSPIRATION"), getcharid(3), 0, 0, 0, 0;
			return 1;
		}
	}

	// 2. MAGO E EVOLUCOES (Inclui Mago T / Mage High)
	if (BaseClass == Job_Mage || Class == Job_Mage || Class == Job_Mage_High || BaseJob == Job_Mage || BaseJob == Job_Mage_High) {
		if (getskilllv("MG_ENERGYCOAT") <= 0) {
			skill "MG_ENERGYCOAT", 1, 0;
		}
		if (Sp >= 30 && !getstatus(SC_ENERGYCOAT) && .@now >= @BUFF_MG_ENERGYCOAT + 60) {
			@BUFF_MG_ENERGYCOAT = .@now;
